Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

For the inductive step, we decompose the organic structure using the extension principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 657. Each subproblem has a solution by the extension principle fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
